The weakened
u
Hat
merges intersected types across the create/delete/exists clauses and drops or weakens predicates such as
\mathit{isDir}(\mathit{content})
on paths, paralleling the weakened study in the evaluation.

Graph

Consider a dynamic graph with fresh nodes and directed edges. Two edges
x\!\to\!y
and
y\!\to\!z
are added (with side conditions on
\mathit{isStart}
). A bug in the underlying program can only be detected on a connected graph, which is captured by the following global property:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ed}=y\land\neg\,\mathit{isStart}(\mathit{st})}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=y\land\mathit{ed}=z}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt isConnReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt isConnRsp}}~|~{\mathit{isConn}}}{\rangle}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt init}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt init}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt newNReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt init}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{nid}=x\land\mathit{isStart}(x)}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\neg(\mathit{nid}=x)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{nid}=x\land\neg\,\mathit{isStart}(x)}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{ed}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}y{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{x\neq y}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ed}=y\land\neg\,\mathit{isStart}(y)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ed}=y}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt isConnReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt isConnReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt isConnRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt isConnRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle v{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt bool}}\,|\,{\mathit{isConn}=v}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt isConnRsp}}~|~{\mathit{isConn}=v}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

The
u
Hat
requires a node is either the initial node, or connected from another node. The weakened
u
Hat
merges two intersected types of
\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}
and loosens
\mathit{nid}/\mathit{isStart}
constraints.

NFA

This benchmark has a similar setting to
Graph
but requires the graph to be a well-formed NFA with at least three edges. Then, the global property is:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt isNFARsp}}~|~{\mathit{isNFA}}}{\rangle}

The
u
Hat
is:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt init}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt init}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt newNReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt init}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\neg(\mathit{nid}=x)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{ed}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt setInitN}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt setInitN}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt setInitN}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt setFinalN}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt setFinalN}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt setFinalN}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}c{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{\mathsf{char}}\,|\,{{\tt true}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}y{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{x\neq y}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{ed}=x}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ch}=c\land\mathit{ed}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ch}=c}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}c{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{\mathsf{char}}\,|\,{{\tt true}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}y{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{x\neq y}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt setInitN}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ch}=c\land\mathit{ed}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ch}=c}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}c{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{\mathsf{char}}\,|\,{{\tt true}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}y{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{x\neq y}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}~|~{\mathit{nid}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{ed}=x}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ch}=c\land\mathit{ed}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\mathit{st}=x\land\mathit{ch}=c}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt isNFAReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt isNFAReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt isNFARsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt isNFARsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle v{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt setFinalN}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt bool}}\,|\,{\mathit{isNFA}=v}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

The weakened
u
Hat
merges intersected types of
\boldsymbol{{\tt newNRsp}}
and
\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}
, and relaxes ordering constraints on
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addE}}~|~{\cdot}}{\rangle}
.

IFC

As introduced in
Sec. 2
, we would like to test the ENNI property, where we are interested in the implementation of the IFC machine over different operators; thus, the global properties vary the offending primitive (
\boldsymbol{{\tt store}}/\boldsymbol{{\tt add}}/\boldsymbol{{\tt load}}
) across three goals.
Then, the global properties are

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt store}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt enniRsp}}~|~{\mathit{enni}}}{\rangle}

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt add}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt enniRsp}}~|~{\mathit{enni}}}{\rangle}

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt load}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt enniRsp}}~|~{\mathit{enni}}}{\rangle}

The
u
Hat
is defined as (here
\mathit{addr}
abbreviates
\mathit{isAddr}
):

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt push}}~:~{}

\displaystyle l{:}{\tt bool}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}x{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{{\tt true}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}y{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{{\tt true}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt push}}\;{\mathit{low}\;\mathit{e_{l}}\;\mathit{e_{r}}}~|~{\mathit{low}=l\land\mathit{e_{l}}=x\land\mathit{e_{r}}=y\land\mathit{addr}(\mathit{e_{l}})\land\mathit{addr}(\mathit{e_{r}})}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=1}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle d{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}l{:}{\tt bool}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}x{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{{\tt true}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}y{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{{\tt true}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt push}}\;{\mathit{low}\;\mathit{e_{l}}\;\mathit{e_{r}}}~|~{\mathit{low}=l\land\mathit{e_{l}}=x\land\mathit{e_{r}}=y\land\mathit{addr}(\mathit{e_{l}})\land\mathit{addr}(\mathit{e_{r}})}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d+1}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt pop}}~:~{}

\displaystyle d{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{d>0}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d\land d>0}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt pop}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d-1}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt load}}~:~{}

\displaystyle d{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{d>0}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt store}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt push}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d\land d>0}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt load}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt store}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt store}}~:~{}

\displaystyle d{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{d>1}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt push}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d\land d>1}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt store}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d-2}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle d{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{d>1}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d\land d>1}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt store}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d-2}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt add}}~:~{}

\displaystyle d{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{d>1}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d\land d>1}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt add}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~|~{\mathit{depth}=d-1}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt enniReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt enniReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt enniRsp}}~|~{\mathit{enni}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt enniRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle v{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt bool}}\,|\,{\mathit{enni}=v}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}~:~{}

\displaystyle d{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{depth}=d}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

Here we use a ghost event
\boldsymbol{{\tt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}depth}}}
to record the current depth of the IFC stack.
The weakened
u
Hat
relaxes the
\mathit{low}/\mathit{e_{l}}/\mathit{e_{r}}
coupling on
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt push}}\;{\cdots}~|~{\cdots}}{\rangle}
toward weaker implications and merges the intersected types of the two
\boldsymbol{{\tt store}}
clauses under looser stack-depth premises.

DeBruijn STLC

The setting of this benchmark is already discussed in the beginning of this appendix (cf.
Appendix B
). The weakened
u
Hat
removes all type-related constraints to enable the generator to produce all untyped STLC programs.

Hashtable

Consider a string-keyed hash table with find/replace that is accessible to multiple threads; we want to detect the concurrency error. Concretely, there is a situation that, after
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k,\mathit{vl}=v_{2}}}{\rangle}
with no further
\boldsymbol{{\tt addReq}}/\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}
on
k
, a
\boldsymbol{{\tt findRsp}}
may still disagree with
v_{2}
—the property captures that mismatch. Then, the global property is defined as:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{vl}=v_{2}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt findRsp}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\neg(\mathit{vl}=v_{2})}}{\rangle}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt addReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle k{:}{\tt int},\;v{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt initReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{vl}=v}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt findReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle k{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{vl}=v_{1}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt rmReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt clReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt findReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt findRsp}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{vl}=v_{1}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle k{:}{\tt int},\;v{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt rmReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{vl}=v}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

The weakened
u
Hat
shortens
\boldsymbol{{\tt findReq}}
/
\boldsymbol{{\tt repReq}}
precondition chains substantially.

Transaction

As explained in the read atomicity example in
Sec. 2
.

DataBase Setting

The four benchmarks below share the same assumption about the underlying database. We adopt the weak isolation levels of MonkeyDB
[
Biswas et al. 2021
]
, which exposes four APIs:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}:{\tt unit}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\tt tid}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}:{\tt tid}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\tt key}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\tt value}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\tt unit}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}:{\tt tid}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\tt cid}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}:{\tt tid}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\tt key}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\tt tid}\times{\tt cid}\times{\tt value}

Calling
\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}
starts a new transaction, and the engine returns a fresh transaction identifier. Each of
\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}
,
\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}
, and
\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}
takes that
{\tt tid}
so the store can tell which transaction is running. The
\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}
operation updates the value stored at a key in the usual way. The
\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}
operation closes the transaction named by the supplied id; the engine assigns a monotonic
commit id

{\tt cid}
that records commit order. For
\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}
, the result bundles the read value with the (
{\tt tid}
,
{\tt cid}
) pair of the writer whose commit produced it; tracking
{\tt cid}
is what lets us state weak isolation. Under causal consistency (CC), for example, reads must be justified by a transaction that commits before the reader commits. Concretely, CC is captured by:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}\geq i}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}k{:}{\tt Key}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}z{:}{\tt Value}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{vl}=z}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~:~{}

\displaystyle j{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}i{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\lor\mathit{cid}\geq j}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\lor\mathit{cid}\geq j}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{cid}=j}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle(\texttt{Causal Consistency:})\hskip 9.24994pt

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~:~{}

\displaystyle p{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}q{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}z{:}{\tt Value}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}i{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}k{:}{\tt Key}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}\;{p\;k\;z}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}\;{p\;q}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{prevTid}=p\land\mathit{prevCid}=q\land\mathit{vl}=z}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{cid}<p}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

These clauses force
{\tt tid}
and
{\tt cid}
to increase monotonically, allow
\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}
only after the matching
\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}
, and pair commits with transactions. Weak isolation shows up chiefly in
\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}
: its history automaton may justify a read from any earlier committed writer, not necessarily the latest. If the current transaction
T_{i}
observes writer
T_{p}
at commit id
q
, the future automaton blocks committing
T_{i}
with any
{\tt cid}<q
, so
T_{i}
cannot commit ahead of
T_{p}
. Read committed (RC) keeps the same surface API but weakens the
u
Hat
for
\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}
:

\displaystyle(\texttt{Read Committed:})\hskip 9.24994pt

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~:~{}

\displaystyle p{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}q{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}z{:}{\tt Value}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}i{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}k{:}{\tt Key}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}\;{p\;k\;z}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=k}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}\;{p\;q}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{prevTid}=p\land\mathit{prevCid}=q\land\mathit{vl}=z}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

Here the history forces
T_{p}
to be the latest committed writer on the key (nothing commits after
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}\;{p\;q}}{\rangle}
). Unlike CC, the future adds no further restriction. In the four benchmarks below we take CC as the default
u
Hat
; weakening yields RC.

Shopping cart

This is a benchmark from MonkeyDB
[
Biswas et al. 2021
]
, which is a simple shopping cart system. The system allows users to add and delete items to their cart, and the cart is stored in the database. The global property is committing
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x,\mathit{vl}=y}}{\rangle}
should rule out a later
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\cdot}}{\rangle}
that reads a stale list (
\neg(\mathit{vl}=y)
), that is

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x\land\neg(\mathit{vl}=y)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses (
\mathit{emp}
,
\mathit{cons}
, and
\mathit{remove}
are the axiomatized list operations):

\displaystyle(\texttt{Causal Consistency Specification is omitted})\hskip 9.24994pt

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt addItemReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}l{:}{{\tt int}}\;{\tt list}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}y{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addItemReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=x\land\mathit{item}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=l}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=\mathit{cons}(y,l)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt addItemRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt addItemRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt deleteItemReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}l{:}{{\tt int}}\;{\tt list}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}y{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt deleteItemReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=x\land\mathit{item}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=l}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=\mathit{remove}(y,l)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt deleteItemRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt deleteItemRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

These clauses encode the underlying database steps used by
\boldsymbol{{\tt addItem}}
and
\boldsymbol{{\tt deleteItem}}
: read the prior cart (a list of item ids) and write back an update. The
{\bullet^{*}}
gaps between those operations admit other trace events (e.g.
\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}
from concurrent transactions). They also forbid
two uncommitted transactions from updating the same key
—the same “write-write conflict” that InnoDB-style engines reject. The constraint appears in the future as the pattern “
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x\land\ldots}}{\rangle}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\ldots}}{\rangle}
”: after one transaction touches key
x
, no second transaction may write
x
until the first commits. For the weakened
u
Hat
, we switch the MonkeyDB layer to RC as above; the cart operations themselves are unchanged.

Courseware

This is a benchmark from MonkeyDB
[
Biswas et al. 2021
]
, where courseware is a system allow student to add and drop courses. The global property is the violation of strong consistency:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x\land\neg(\mathit{vl}=y)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

The
u
Hat
is defined as follows (
\mathit{emp}
,
\mathit{cons}
, and
\mathit{remove}
are as in the Shopping cart benchmark):

\displaystyle(\texttt{Causal Consistency Specification is omitted})\hskip 9.24994pt

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt regStuReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt regStuReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}\cup{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}\cup{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt regStuReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{emp}(\mathit{vl})}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt regStuRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt regStuReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt regStuRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt crtCourseReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int},\;x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt crtCourseReq}}~|~{\mathit{course\_id}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putCourses}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt crtCourseRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt crtCourseRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt enrStuReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}l{:}{{\tt int}}\;{\tt list}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}y{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt enrStuReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=x\land\mathit{item}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=l}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=\mathit{cons}(y,l)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt enrStuRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt enrStuRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt unenrStuReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}l{:}{{\tt int}}\;{\tt list}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}y{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt unenrStuReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=x\land\mathit{item}=y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=l}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{key}=x\land\mathit{vl}=\mathit{remove}(y,l)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{key}=x}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt unenrStuRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt unenrStuRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

These clauses encode the underlying database steps used by courseware’s API, which is similar to the
Shopping
benchmark. They also forbid
two uncommitted transactions from updating the same key
—the same “write-write conflict” that InnoDB-style engines reject. For the weakened
u
Hat
, we switch the MonkeyDB layer to RC as above; the cart operations themselves are unchanged.

Twitter

This is a benchmark from MonkeyDB
[
Biswas et al. 2021
]
, modeling social follow sets and timelines on top of the transactional store. The global property is a violation of strong consistency: after observing follows list
\ell
for user
u
, with no further
\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}
between, a later
\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}
may read a different set.

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{fls}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u\land\neg(\mathit{fls}=\ell)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

The
u
Hat
is defined as follows (
\mathit{emp}
,
\mathit{cons}
, and
\mathit{remove}
are as in the Shopping cart benchmark):

\displaystyle(\texttt{Causal Consistency Specification is omitted})\hskip 9.24994pt

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt followReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int},{\,\dashrightarrow\,}\ell{:}{{\tt int}}\;{\tt list}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}u{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}f{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt followReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{follow\_o}=f}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{fls}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{fls}=\mathit{cons}(f,\ell)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt followRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt followRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt unfollowReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int},{\,\dashrightarrow\,}\ell{:}{{\tt int}}\;{\tt list}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}u{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}f{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt unfollowReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{unfollow\_o}=f}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt get}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{fls}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{fls}=\mathit{remove}(f,\ell)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt unfollowRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt unfollowRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt postTweetReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int},{\,\dashrightarrow\,}\ell{:}{{\tt int}}\;{\tt list}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}u{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}f{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt postTweetReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{tweet}=\tau}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt getT}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{tweets}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putT}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{tweets}=\mathit{cons}(\tau,\ell)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putT}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt postTweetRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle i{:}{\tt int},{\,\dashrightarrow\,}\ell{:}{{\tt int}}\;{\tt list}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}u{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt timelineReq}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt getT}}~|~{\mathit{tid}=i\land\mathit{user}=u\land\mathit{tweets}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

These specifications mirror the Shopping-style pattern: each API issues
\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}/\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}
around reads and updates of the underlying rows, with
{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt put}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}
/
{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putT}}~|~{\mathit{user}=u}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}
forbidding write–write conflicts on those projections. For the weakened
u
Hat
, we switch the MonkeyDB layer to RC as above; the Twitter operations themselves are unchanged.

SmallBank

This is a benchmark from MonkeyDB
[
Biswas et al. 2021
]
, implementing the SmallBank workload (accounts, checking, and savings) on the transactional store. The global property is a violation of strong consistency: two reads of checking balance
b
for customer
c
with no
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putC}}~|~{\mathit{cm}=c}}{\rangle}
in between should agree, yet a later read may disagree.

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt getC}}~|~{\mathit{cm}=c\land\mathit{bal}=b}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putC}}~|~{\mathit{cm}=c}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt getC}}~|~{\mathit{cm}=c\land\neg(\mathit{bal}=b)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

The
u
Hat
is defined as follows:

\displaystyle(\texttt{Causal Consistency Specification is omitted})\hskip 9.24994pt

Each scenario threads
\boldsymbol{{\tt begin}}/\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}
with the same write–write gaps on the relevant account rows (e.g.
{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putC}}~|~{\mathit{cm}=c}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}
before commit) as in
