. For the weakened
u
Hat
, we switch the MonkeyDB layer to RC as above; the SmallBank scenarios themselves are unchanged.

P Language Benchmarks

The remaining models are asynchronous message-passing protocols drawn from PBench. As in
Stack
and the synchronous benchmarks above, we give a short scenario, the global property as an SRE, the operation-wise
u
Hat
clauses (where futures often encode protocol order and search bias), and a note on weakening.

Database

This model is a simplified database with APIs:
\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}/\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}
and
\boldsymbol{{\tt readReq}}/\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}
. It must satisfy the Read-Your-Writes (RYW) policy: reads should respect the last completed write unless another write slips in between; the generator therefore chases a successful read that nevertheless returns
y\neq x
right after
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}
and with no further
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}
in the gap. Thus, the global property is:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=y\land x\neq y\land\mathit{st}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt readReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x\land\mathit{st}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\neg\mathit{st}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{va}=x}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}s{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{va}=x\land\mathit{st}=s}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

The history on
\boldsymbol{{\tt readReq}}
ties a successful read to a matching prior
\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}
(the Kleene gap excludes stray writes), which biases generation toward read-your-writes-shaped traces. The weakened
u
Hat
relaxes those payload ties on
\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}
and
\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}
by weakening the qualifier directly.

Firewall

The firewall divides the nodes into “internal nodes” and “external nodes”; external nodes cannot send messages to internal nodes until they are recorded on the “white list” of the firewall. To add an external node to the white list, at least one internal node must send it a message. The firewall under test has incorrect white list recording, which leads to an error described by the following global property:

\displaystyle{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eExReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eFwReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eExRsp}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n\land\neg\,\mathit{stat}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eStart}}~:~{}

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eStart}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eExReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eFwReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eFwReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eFwReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eFwReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eExReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eExReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eExReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eExRsp}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n\land\mathit{stat}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}~|~{\neg(\mathit{node}=n)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eExReq}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eExRsp}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n\land\neg\mathit{stat}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eExRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}s{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt bool}}\,|\,{\mathit{stat}=s}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

Histories on
\boldsymbol{{\tt eInReq}}
and
\boldsymbol{{\tt eFwReq}}
rule out re-entrant forwarding noise before the next hop, sharpening the causal shape the tester must satisfy. The weakened
u
Hat
merges the intersected
\boldsymbol{{\tt eExReq}}
cases and weakens guards on those events.

RingLeaderElection

Processes ring-elect a leader using
\boldsymbol{{\tt eWakeup}}
and
\boldsymbol{{\tt eNom}}
, with outcomes published as
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}~|~{\mathit{ld}=\cdots}}{\rangle}
; at most one leader value should ultimately win. The search targets two
\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}
announcements that contradict each other on
\mathit{ld}
. The global property is:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}~|~{\mathit{ld}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}~|~{\neg(\mathit{ld}=\ell)}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses (
\mathit{next}
is axiomatized on ring nodes):

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eWakeup}}~:~{}

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eNom}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n\land\mathit{ld}=n}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eNom}}~:~{}

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}\ell{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{\mathsf{Node}}\,|\,{\ell\neq n}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eNom}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n\land\mathit{ld}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}~|~{\mathit{ld}=\ell}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle n{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}\ell{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{\mathsf{Node}}\,|\,{\ell=n}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eNom}}~|~{\mathit{node}=n\land\mathit{ld}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eNom}}~|~{\mathit{node}=\mathit{next}(n)\land\mathit{ld}=\ell}}{\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}~:~{}

\displaystyle\ell{:}\mathsf{Node}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}~|~{\mathit{ld}=\ell}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

The future on
\boldsymbol{{\tt eWakeup}}
forbids any earlier
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}
, steering traces toward elections that have not yet closed; that is the main search bias for this benchmark. The weakened
u
Hat
drops these guard constraints on
\boldsymbol{{\tt eWakeup}}
and
\boldsymbol{{\tt eWon}}
.

Bankserver

The bank server sequences balance reads and updates around
\boldsymbol{{\tt wdReq}}
and
\boldsymbol{{\tt eWDRsp}}
; withdrawals are meant to stay within available funds. The buggy server still reports a failed withdrawal on
\mathit{ac}
(
\neg\mathit{status}
) in a situation where the recorded balances should have sufficed. The global property is:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWDRsp}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=\mathit{ac}\land\neg\,\mathit{status}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses (
\mathsf{aid}/\mathsf{rid}
are phantom account/request ids):

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt initAc}}~:~{}

\displaystyle ac{:}\mathsf{aid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ba{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{ba>0}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt initAc}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=ac}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt initAc}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt wdReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle id{:}\mathsf{rid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ac{:}\mathsf{aid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}am{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{am>0}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt wdReq}}~|~{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{amt}=am}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt read}}~|~{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{amt}=am}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt read}}~:~{}

\displaystyle ba{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}id{:}\mathsf{rid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}am{:}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ac{:}\mathsf{aid}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt initAc}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt read}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=ac}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt initAc}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=ac}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt read}}~|~{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{amt}=am\land\mathit{acId}=ac}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{amt}=am\land\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle id{:}\mathsf{rid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}am{:}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ac{:}\mathsf{aid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ba{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{ba>0\land ba>am}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt read}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt wdReq}}~|~{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{amt}=am}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt wdReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt update}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba-am}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWDRsp}}~|~{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba-am\land\mathit{status}}}{\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle id{:}\mathsf{rid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}am{:}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ac{:}\mathsf{aid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ba{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{ba>0\land ba\leq am}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{amt}=am\land\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eWDRsp}}~|~{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{acId}=ac\land\neg\mathit{status}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt update}}~:~{}

\displaystyle ac{:}\mathsf{aid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ba{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{ba>0}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt update}}~|~{\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eWDRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle id{:}\mathsf{rid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ac{:}\mathsf{aid}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}ba{:}{\tt int},\;st{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt unit}}\,|\,{\mathit{rId}=id\land\mathit{acId}=ac\land\mathit{bal}=ba\land\mathit{status}=st}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

The successful branch requires
\mathit{bal}>\mathit{amt}
at the read response so the backend can emit
\boldsymbol{{\tt update}}
; the failing branch skips the update. The weakened
u
Hat
simplifies response/update qualifiers and widens read-query intersected types.

Simplified2pc

The SUT is a simplified two-phase commit client: committed state should read atomically, so after a write response commits
x
and no further write completes in the middle, a read response should not return
y\neq x
. In SRE form this is a committed
\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}
, a Kleene stretch with no further successful
\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}
, a
\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}
that mismatches the written value, and a final stretch with no further
\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}
or
\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}
.
The global property is:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x\land\mathit{stat}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{\mathit{stat}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=y\land x\neq y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl(\bullet\setminus({{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}}\cup{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}

The
u
Hat
is:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt readReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt getReq}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt getReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=-1}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{va}=x}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{x\geq 0}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putReq}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt putReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putReq}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt putRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}s{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x\land\mathit{stat}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x\land\mathit{stat}=s}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}s{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt putRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x\land\mathit{stat}=s}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x\land\neg\mathit{stat}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt abort}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~:~{}

\displaystyle x{:}{\tt int}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}s{:}{\tt bool}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@lbrace}}}{}{{\nu}{:}{{\tt int}}\,|\,{\mathit{va}=x\land\mathit{stat}=s}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{\lx@text@rbrace}}^{u}}{}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt commit}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt abort}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt abort}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

The weakened
u
Hat
drops non-negative value constraints on
\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}
; many
putRsp
,
writeRsp
, and
readRsp
payload tests become
true
.

Heartbeat

The heartbeat failure detector should align “nodes down” announcements with actual shutdowns. Note that messages can be lost due to network issues. The system has a timeout retry limit as
3
(i.e.,
\mathit{trial}
). The bug class is a false alarm:
\boldsymbol{{\tt eNotifyNodesDown}}
fires while
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eShutDown}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}
never appears on either side in the exhibited pattern. The global property is:

\displaystyle{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eShutDown}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eNotifyNodesDown}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eShutDown}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses:

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eNotifyNodesDown}}~:~{}

\displaystyle{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eNotifyNodesDown}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{{\bullet^{*}}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt eNetworkError}}~:~{}

u
Hat
keeps the trial-level structure but softens side conditions.

Chainreplication

Chain-replicated storage ought to make completed writes visible to later successful reads on the same key. This chain is simplified to only have three nodes: the head node, middle node, and tail node; since the Chain Replication protocol has three different policies for these three kinds of nodes. Here a client still reads a stale value: the write on
\mathit{key}=k
finishes (
\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}
), yet the next successful read (
\mathit{st}
) returns
y\neq x
although no further
{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}
occurs between them. The global property is:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt readRsp}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{va}=y\land y\neq x\land\mathit{st}}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\bigl((\bullet\setminus{{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeRsp}}~|~{{\tt true}}}{\rangle}})\bigr)^{\ast}}

The
u
Hat
comprises the following clauses (
\mathit{mid1}/\mathit{mid2}
axiomatize mid vs. tail nodes):

\displaystyle\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~:~{}

\displaystyle k{:}\mathsf{Key}{\,\dashrightarrow\,}x{:}{\tt int}{\,\shortrightarrow\,}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\bullet^{*}}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeReq}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt writeToMid}}~|~{\mathit{key}=k\land\mathit{va}=x\land\mathit{mid1}(\mathit{node})}}{\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}

Paxos

Paxos exposes an observable learner
\boldsymbol{{\tt eLearn}}
that ought to settle on a single replicated value. We use
*\boldsymbol{{\tt Lost}}*
to simulate the network issue as introduced in the
HeartBeat
benchmark. The stress trace lets the learner emit two learns with different payloads
x
and
y
(
x\neq y
). The global property is:

\displaystyle{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eLearn}}~|~{\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}{\cdot}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt eLearn}}~|~{\mathit{va}=y\land x\neq y}}{\rangle}{\cdot}{\bullet^{*}}

u
Hat
comprises the following clauses (
\mathit{pr1}/\mathit{pr2}
partition proposers and
\mathit{acc1}/\mathit{acc2}
partition acceptors):

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt ePrepReq}}~|~{\mathit{proposer}=p\land\mathit{acc1}(\mathit{acceptor})\land\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}\,\boldsymbol{{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\sqcap}}\,

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt{\tt unit}\,{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{[}}}{}{\langle}{\boldsymbol{{\tt ePrepReq}}~|~{\mathit{proposer}=p\land\mathit{acc2}(\mathit{acceptor})\land\mathit{va}=x}}{\rangle}{\color[rgb]{0.0195,0.4883,0.0313}\mbox{\rm{]}}}{}
